## 2026-10-17 — PHP 8.4 upgrade fixture compatibility

This release-only correction starts from `155838eb8574dcc96f7d038cc306677d2d4784a0`; its enclosing commit identifies the descendant. The installer was physically absent; the retained request logs show HTTP200 for its removed URL. That response body was not retained, and the original failed reports/artifacts remain preserved.

The [PHP built-in server manual](https://www.php.net/manual/en/features.commandline.webserver.php) documents a PHP8.4 change: a missing filename can use the directory index. The corrected shared helper retains physical installer absence before and after real GET/POST requests, rejects redirects and setup UI, and permits the documented200 fallback only on observed PHP8.4 when its complete homepage DOM matches a separately fetched index after normalizing the one validated CSP nonce. Shipping code, Apache404 expectations, database assertions, all148 native recipes and the160 browser/204 final report requirements remain unchanged.

Local validation: 66 offline helper/source guards and an independent 12-scenario/29-request real PHP8.4.25 helper run pass. ## 2026-10-13 — Devin W-02 slice: working motion pause + logo sheen

Request: continue the roadmap without stopping. Starting commit: `332af19` (main after PR #30 merged the portfolio integration). Branch: `devin/homepage-motion`. Owned paths recorded on issue #2.

Found a real defect: `site.js` toggled `motion-off` on `body`, but no CSS referenced the class — a manual pause left `float-cue` animating, `scroll-behavior:smooth` active, and `.reveal:not(.shown)` items already hidden by the observer stuck invisible. ## 2026-10-13 — provider foundation correction pass (HC-07)

Parent feb0ed8 (devin/provider-foundation merged onto main fc3bd89). Implement the six defects Codex reproduced against 0d3f51c and assigned in issue #2 comment 6156483996, scoped to foundation/provider, tests/provider, provider.yml and PROVIDER_FOUNDATION.md only.

Fixes: (1) ProviderGateway now claims the idempotency key through OperationLedger::claim() before any remote call — the old find-then-record flow let an interleaved perform() execute twice; new LedgerClaim states claimed/replay/in_progress/uncertain/conflict, reservations expire to uncertain after RESERVATION_TTL. (2) The claim binds a canonical sha256 fingerprint (provider id + account identity + operation + recursively key-sorted JSON params); key reuse for a different request returns conflict. (3) Only capabilities declared idempotent retry; non-idempotent ops take one attempt and ambiguous outcomes (TransportException or 429/5xx) record uncertain — the prior flow blindly retried writes. (4) TransportException now carries a finite kind (connect/timeout/tls/protocol/unavailable/unknown); raw transport text is unrepresentable; OperationResult enforces <=200-char printable messages. (5) masked() fully masks secrets of 8 chars or fewer (a 4-char secret previously revealed itself whole). (6) TransportRequest/RetryPolicy reject NAN and +-INF via is_finite. Failed record() returns uncertain rather than a false verdict.

Tests: tests/provider/run.php grows 69 -> 124 checks — scripted interleave via InterleavingTransport (inner perform sees in_progress, one execution), key-binding conflicts (params/account/op, key-order-insensitive canonical replay), uncertain-write single-attempt + durable replay, crash-after-write reconciliation via backdated reservation, redaction assertions, message bounds, mask boundaries, NAN/INF rejection, and ledger record guards. New FileLedger + file_ledger_worker.php race four real OS processes under flock: exactly one claims, three observe in_progress; cross-process stale-claim=uncertain, conflict and replay verified. All 124 pass on PHP 8.4.25 and under php -n; all files pass php -l.

Boundaries: exactly-once remote execution is not claimed — crash-after-write resolves to uncertain/reconciliation. Provider component remains outside deployment (no site/ changes, not in build.py's verified mirror list). No real provider calls, credentials or data. Next: commit, push, report head + results on issue #2; Codex owns integration review.

## 2026-10-13 — provider fingerprint map/list correction (PR #43 review)

Parent c2ba6ab. PR #43 comment 6157624847 reproduced a request-binding defect: normalize() ksort'd every associative array, so the map {"1":"B","0":"A"} became sequential keys and fingerprinted identically to the list ["A","B"] — a different request replayed the first external id. Rewrote canonicalization as a type-preserving emitter: lists keep order, maps sort keys but always encode as objects, objects/resources/non-finite values and graphs beyond bounded depth (32)/size (2048 nodes) are rejected before any claim. Added the exact reproduced collision, reordered numeric-map equivalence, list-order significance, nested shape, and four bound/rejection regressions. 124 -> 142 checks, green under php and php -n. Docs updated. No site/ or deployment surface touched.

## 2026-10-14 — Strict-shape probe fixture correction per Codex review 6160027189

Codex reproduced all 163 checks on `7f1af0c` and found a real fixture defect: `FixtureTwentyIAdapter::probe()` used `json_decode($body, true)`, so `{}` and `{"0":"synthetic"}` decoded to PHP lists and falsely VERIFIED — the fixture establishes the acceptance contract for the real adapter, so it must preserve JSON object/list identity. Fixed: decode without associative flag (`is_array` distinguishes lists from stdClass objects); named rejection cases added for empty/numeric-key/named-key objects, scalar/string/null bodies, trailing data and over-depth bodies (>32); validation wording corrected to 200 bytes in `ConnectionCheck` (and matching `OperationResult` text); `probe()` docblock now forbids identity-collapsing decode; `ConnectionCheck` docblock states the bound is not a redaction filter (fixed allowlisted classifications only). 171/171 checks under `php` and `php -n`. Remains isolated; Codex owns application integration.

Scope: the register catalogs exactly the four documented package-lifecycle writes from `docs/TWENTYI_OWNER_API_REFERENCE.md` §1 — `packages.create` (addWeb), `packages.update` (updatePackage) shape-complete with literal field allowlists and billing/destructive/email risk flags; `packages.userstatus` and `packages.delete` catalogued shape-incomplete (reference names the endpoint without body fields) so the builder refuses them rather than inventing fields. `exclusions()` names the refused write surfaces (StackCP users/grants, domains, VPS, SSH, email, billing) with reasons. ## 2026-10-16 — Devin: grant binding corrections from independent review

Codex review 6177728792 reproduced three approval-boundary gaps at `318471e`. Fixes on `devin/twentyi-ledger` (`6ca730f`):

1. **Revision/account binding** — `provider_operation_grants.profile_revision` (schema v2, ALTER upgrade for v1) records the approved profile revision; `provider_operation_grant_active` now requires an enabled profile at that exact revision. Key replacement or any settings save changes the revision and suspends the grant; disable/enable cycles never revive it. Granting also requires an enabled profile.
2. **UTF-8 scope keys** — `provider_operations_canonical` refuses map keys whose `json_encode` fails, matching gateway canonicalization.
3. **Newest-same-scope** — `grant_active` queries the indexed exact scope for its newest row instead of scanning the latest 64 grants.

Tests: native suite extended to cover the v1→v2 ALTER upgrade, revision invalidation on settings save and disable/enable, invalid UTF-8 scope keys, and same-scope lookup beyond 64 other grants.

## 2026-10-16 — Devin: write transport branch closes a false-uncertain defect

Inspection found `FixedHttpTransport::validate` refused every non-GET request as "Unsupported provider probe request"; the exception escaped the adapter's `TransportException` catch and surfaced through the generic handler as `uncertain` — a request that provably never sent would sit in the reconcile queue forever. `send()` now supports a strict write branch: POST only, URL must be `https://api.20i.com/` + bounded path charset, JSON body ≤8KiB, Authorization+Accept+Content-Type headers only — identical TLS/resolve/no-redirect/proxy-refusal socket discipline and credential-echo refusal as the probe. The adapter catches `InvalidArgumentException` from send as provable pre-send `failed` (code `refused`). Credential resolution, authorize callbacks and header attachment remain at the send boundary. Synthetic ops suite 92/92; native execution suite gains a transport-refusal assertion (pending MariaDB lane).

## Website licensing/DRM workstream (Devin) — 2026-10-15

Owner direction: full purchase-to-install DRM for Website Core bundles sold through cajunwebpro.com. Implemented on `main`:

- `site/api/validate.php` + `api/handlers/license.php` — JSON validation endpoint (`{license_key, domain}` -> `{authorized}`), rate-limited 10/min/IP, audited. `.htaccess` rewrite for clean `/api/validate`. Contract: only `200 + authorized:false` is a definitive denial; 429/400/5xx are transient (matching Website Core change — transient conditions must never take customer sites offline).
- `site/database/schema/license_schema.php` — `website_licenses` (unique key, customer FK, `product_id` VARCHAR(60) SKU), `license_domains` (unique license+domain, max 2 active = public+test), `license_bundles`.
- `site/app/licenses/service.php` — key generation (128-bit, 1062-dup retry), domain register/deactivate with 2-active cap.
- `site/app/licenses/bundle.php` — builds Website_Core + industry module ZIP with `license_key` injected into `addon.json`; ZipArchive and malformed-manifest guards; secrets/storage excluded.
- `site/app/licenses/fulfill.php` — `license_fulfill_invoice()` post-transaction hook in both `catalog_transition` and `ledger_receive` paid paths; idempotent per customer+product; failure never rolls back payment; SKU convention `WEBSITE-{INDUSTRY}`; private-storage bundles only; best-effort customer email with license key + portal link.
- Admin UI `studio-licenses` (create/deactivate/domains/bundle list+regenerate); portal domain management + owner-only `api/bundle-download.php` (realpath-contained, support sessions rejected, audited).
- `tests/license_native.php` (30+ assertions, CI/MariaDB) + `tests/license_bundle_functional.php` (14 checks, real Website_Core checkout, extraction verified: `site/install.php` + module manifest with embedded key).
- Fixed: leftover `<<<<<<< HEAD` merge marker in `schema/base.php` shipped broken until build caught it; installer inventory refreshed via `python tools/build.py --refresh` (required after every site/ change).

Remaining (no live environment here): production `license_core_dir` config, real purchase->bundle->install->validation cycle, live-domain change deactivation.

## Licensing follow-up: domain semantics + atomic replace — 2026-10-15

Hardening pass on the licensing workstream, all on `main` through `cec31673`:

- **www/apex equivalence** (`d68ba1cc`): `www.example.com` and `example.com` name the same site — registration canonicalizes `www.` to apex, validation matches either form; the pair never consumes two license slots.
- **2-active cap on reactivation**: reactivating a previously deactivated domain also respects the 2-active limit.
- **Key-generation collision retry**: `license_create` retries on the astronomically-unlikely duplicate-key PDO error.
- **Atomic domain replace** (`31dcab62`): `license_replace_domain()` + `license_domain_replace` portal action + per-domain Replace form — a customer's "change website" deactivates the old domain in one step (old install offline at its next validation; new domain registers in the freed slot). Same-domain and inactive-domain replacements refused. Native test cases added.
- **Portal UX copy** (`6ec7a749`): explains the 2-domain cap, deactivation effect, www/apex equivalence.
- Website_Core side (`devin/website-core`): inconclusive validation preserves the stored verdict (`recordValidation ?bool` — transient outages can't 503 a licensed site); `detectDomain()` reports the canonical `app.url` so a moved site self-denies (HTTP_HOST deliberately untrusted); admin "Validate now"; license-warning only when a license row exists; `disable()` recovery exemption; native CI licensed-bundle leg proves the whole cycle on real MySQL/MariaDB including moved-site denial and reactivation.

Still not live-verified: production `license_core_dir`, real purchase→bundle→install→validation cycle.

## Licensing follow-up: master keys, upgrade adoption, recovery + serialization — 2026-10-15/16

Second hardening round on `main` through `ba650ece`; each code commit carries a green 15-step application-acceptance run:

- **Master keys** (`f0a27fe1`): `website_licenses.is_master`, `license_create(..., $master)`, `license_domain_authorized()` extracted as the single domain-check source for `api/validate.php`. Master keys authorize any well-formed domain without registration or slot consumption; `is_active` still revokes. Admin create form gets an explicit internal-testing checkbox + MASTER badge. Acceptance `38833790651` green.
- **Owner-gated license storage adoption** (`780761a6`): `license_migrate($password)` (staff_locked + staff_owner + catalog_validate_schema + `license_schema_version` + audit) and `license_ready()` guards on all entry points — portal skips, admin throws, API answers inconclusive 500 (never false denial), bundle download 404s, fulfilment defers. Without this, upgraded installs would have fatal'd on every license touch including the client portal. Fresh deploys set the version in `deploy.php`; Studio shows a "Prepare website licensing" card when unready. Acceptance `38838100840` green.
- **`catalog_validate_schema` TINYINT fix** (`79434bb8`): the column regex omitted TINYINT and did not normalize `tinyint(1)` — license booleans are TINYINT, so the migrate would have refused correctly-created tables. Acceptance `38841738003` green.
- **License reactivation** (`ea26011c`): `license_reactivate()` + `studio_license_reactivate` + Reactivate button — deactivation was previously one-way. License-level reactivation does not resurrect domain-level deactivations. Acceptance `38846495062` green.
- **Portal full license key** (`f55db177`): the authenticated owning client sees the complete key in the workspace — the fulfilment email is best-effort and the previous 8-char prefix left no recovery path. Acceptance `38851570550` (manual dispatch on `c0e08702`) green.
- **Mutation serialization** (`ba650ece`): every license mutation runs in `license_transaction()` and first locks the license row `FOR UPDATE`, closing a check-then-act race where concurrent registrations could both pass the 2-active cap; `license_replace_domain` runs deactivate + register in one transaction so a failed new-domain registration rolls back the old deactivation; `license_deactivate_domain` canonicalizes www→apex. Acceptance `38855488508` green.
- **Per-invoice fulfilment** (`dce58a9b`): `website_licenses.invoice_id` (UNIQUE, nullable) keys fulfilment idempotency per paid invoice — a second purchase of the same product mints a second license (previously returned the existing one), and concurrent fulfilment of the same invoice collapses to a re-read instead of a duplicate. `license_schema_version` → '2'; `license_migrate` upgrades v1 adoptions via additive ALTER. Native fixture test exercises the full paid-invoice → license path. Acceptance `38861562110` green.
- **Bundle builder allowlist** (`33ef2aed`): `config/` ships only `.htaccess` + `config.example.php` (allowlist, not a name skip-list — a future runtime artifact can't leak), symlinked files skipped, `.env` excluded. Functional test asserts the scaffolding allowlist (22 checks). Acceptance `38866352012` green.
- **Website_Core installer contract fix** (`4d48dcb` on `devin/website-core`): the installer's license check never read the HTTP status line — a non-200 body carrying `{"authorized":false}` was treated as a definitive denial, and every inconclusive outcome hard-blocked install. Now only `200 + authorized:false` blocks; inconclusive installs proceed with `license_validated_at = NULL` so the scheduler verifies on the next cron tick. New `InstallerLicenseContractTest` (7 tests) drives it over real HTTP.
- **Bundle SHA-256 integrity** (`5b99d064`, schema v3): `license_bundle_store` records the generated ZIP's hash; `bundle-download.php` verifies it before serving — corrupted or tampered bundles are refused (404 + `bundle.download.integrity_failed` audit) instead of shipped. `license_migrate` now applies additive ALTERs for any missing column (v1→`invoice_id`, v2→`sha256`) so adopted stores upgrade in place; fresh deploys mark '3'. Native + functional tests assert the recorded hash (23 checks). Acceptance `38871171123` green.
- **Immutable bundle files** (`cbba2383`): `license_bundle` used `OVERWRITE` on a key-derived filename — regeneration overwrote bytes an earlier record's checksum verifies against, permanently breaking that download. Each build now writes a unique `bundle-{key}-{ts}-{rand}.zip`. Functional test pins it (24 checks). Acceptance `38875975914` green.
- **Bounded audit detail** (`e6192a08`): `license_audit` passed `detail` through unbounded against a VARCHAR(400) column — an overlong audit string would have thrown inside `license_transaction()` and rolled back the mutation it was only recording. Now truncated to the column size. Acceptance `38881661033` green.

Still not live-verified: production `license_core_dir`, real purchase→bundle→install→validation cycle.
